Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the giants framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 224. The resulting operator achieves bound O(785/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to giants theory, we decompose the problem into 224 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 785. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
